Single well-mixed compartment; no vascular, glomerular or medullary geometry.

Filtered and reabsorbed sodium are reduced budget quantities, not a full nephron solver.

Active fraction and ATP stoichiometry are fixed scenario assumptions; basal metabolism is excluded.

Viability box [13.5,14.5] L and [135,145] mmol/L is a chosen teaching constraint, not a survival threshold.

Delayed and scrambled sensors are synthetic causal interventions, not empirical neural mechanisms.

Equal resource budget, different signal-to-action correspondence

synthetic_or_conditional_teaching_experiment

channel information bits

0.5310044064107188

fixed decoder expected accuracy

0.9

optimally remapped expected accuracy

0.9

strategymatched_fractionallocation_energy_Jobservation_countrecovered_unitsunrecovered_units
matched0.89500.05001780220
fixed0.5500.050010001000
shuffled0.534500.05001068932
delayed0.26500.05005201480
reversed0.11500.05002201780
calibrated_remap0.89500.05001780220

Each strategy receives the same number of observations and spends 1 J per action by construction.

Allocation energy excludes sensing and computation; this is a matched-action-budget experiment, not a total thermodynamic audit.

Calibrated remapping knows the declared error rate. It is not inferred from the test sequence.

Toy correspondence experiment; no conclusion about a new physical information field or consciousness follows.

An explicit hidden state changes a repeated response

synthetic_or_conditional_teaching_experiment

history state initial

0.8

h(t)=h0 exp(-lambda t); response=s(t)(1+h(t)). The history variable is assumed, not inferred from a single endpoint.

This toy cannot distinguish epigenetic state, persistent exposure and measurement bias without additional observations.

Functional recovery and reserve recovery can separate

synthetic_or_conditional_teaching_experiment

functional deficit at 180

0.0007465858083766792

structural deficit at 180

0.4065696597405991

reserve at 180

0.8373721361037604

Independent exponential recovery modes selected for demonstration; not a disease-course model.

A shared measured endpoint does not demonstrate identical structure or capacity for a second challenge.

Parameters, full result and source sections
{

The sinusoid is an arbitrary added effect with a declared basis, not an information-field equation.

Baseline constants, noise variance, basis and split are fixed in advance; only the extension amplitude is fitted on even-index samples.

Held-out performance can discriminate these two candidates under this generator; it cannot establish a novel physical field.
